### 1.2 External Actors & Systems

| Actor/System | Type | Description | Interaction Protocol |
|-------------|------|-------------|---------------------|
| Security Guards | Person | Field personnel performing patrols, responding to incidents | Mobile App (Flutter), HTTPS REST API |
| Site Supervisors | Person | Manage guard teams, review incidents, monitor sites | Web Portal (React), GraphQL API |
| Operations Managers | Person | Oversee contracts, analyze performance, schedule resources | Web Portal, ERPNext ERP |
| HR Officers | Person | Manage employee records, training, certifications | OrangeHRM, Moodle LMS |
| System Administrators | Person | Maintain infrastructure, deploy updates, monitor security | SSH, Kubernetes CLI, Proxmox UI |
| Executives/CISO | Person | Strategic oversight, compliance review, analytics | Grafana dashboards (read-only) |
| Clients | Person | View site activity, request services, review reports | Client Portal (React), Mobile App |
| IoT Sensors | System | Environmental monitoring, checkpoint validation | MQTT, LoRaWAN |
| CCTV Cameras | System | Video surveillance, computer vision analytics | RTSP, HTTP, ZoneMinder API |
| Access Control | System | Door locks, badge readers, intercoms | MQTT, HTTP, OpenHAB integration |
| Nurse Call Systems | External | Hospital emergency alerting (healthcare sector) | HL7 FHIR API, Node-RED bridge |
| Building Management | External | HVAC, fire suppression, elevator control | BACnet, Modbus TCP |
| Provincial Licensing DB | External | Guard license verification | REST API (where available) |
| Payment Gateways | External | Client invoicing, payroll processing | Stripe API, bank ACH |

### 1.3 Key Quality Attributes

- **Security**: Zero-trust architecture, mTLS everywhere, encrypted data at rest and in transit
- **Availability**: 99.9% uptime SLA, edge computing for site autonomy during WAN outages
- **Scalability**: Kubernetes auto-scaling, horizontal pod scaling, distributed storage (Ceph)
- **Compliance**: SOC 2 Type II, HIPAA, PIPEDA, NIST SP 800-53 Rev 5 controls
- **Auditability**: Immutable logs (Wazuh), blockchain-anchored certificates (LibreSign)
- **Interoperability**: Open standards (REST, GraphQL, MQTT, RTSP), federation support (Matrix)

### 2.3 Key Interactions

| Source | Target | Protocol/Method | Purpose |
|--------|--------|----------------|---------|
| Ansible | Proxmox API | HTTPS REST | Automated VM/container provisioning |
| Kubernetes | Proxmox nodes | SSH/KVM | Node management and resource allocation |
| All services | Ceph cluster | RADOS, RBD, CephFS | Distributed storage access |
| Edge sites | Primary DC | WireGuard (UDP 51820) | Encrypted site-to-site VPN |
| pfSense | Suricata | Netmap/AF_PACKET | Inline IDS/IPS packet inspection |
| Monitoring | All nodes | Prometheus exporter (HTTP 9100) | Metrics collection |
| Backup system | All VMs | SSH + BorgBackup | Encrypted incremental backups |

---

### 3.3 Key Security Patterns

| Pattern | Implementation | Purpose |
|---------|---------------|---------|
| Zero Trust | All connections require mTLS cert validation | Never trust, always verify |
| Defense in Depth | Multiple security layers (network, host, application) | Redundant protection |
| Least Privilege | RBAC with minimum required permissions | Limit blast radius |
| Immutable Audit | Wazuh logs write-once to OpenSearch | Tamper-proof forensics |
| Secrets Rotation | Vault dynamic secrets with TTL | Reduce credential exposure window |
| Network Segmentation | VLANs enforced by pfSense + mTLS service mesh | Lateral movement prevention |

---

Key Integration Patterns:
1. Real-time: Kafka events for operational data (patrols, incidents)
2. Scheduled: Nightly ETL jobs (Airflow) for analytics aggregation
3. On-demand: REST API for external client portal queries
4. Federated: LDAP for user authentication/authorization

â”‚  â”‚  Training: Nightly retraining via Airflow DAG                        â”‚  â”‚
